みずほ経済 EXPRESS

日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ 
～物価上振れリスクが強まれば、一段の利上げ加速も～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
池田 亮平 
エコノミスト 阿部 大樹 
シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山 祐資 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 日銀は物価上振れ抑制に舵。中東情勢に起因する物価押し上げリスクは予断を許さず 
9 月日銀会合後の会見で植田総裁は「政策局面が変化した」と述べた（図表 1） 。これまで日銀は、
基調的物価の押し上げを金融政策運営の重点としてきたが、足元では 2％前後の物価上昇が定着しつ
つあるとみられる。こうしたもとで、金融政策運営の重点は物価上振れの回避に移っており、そのた
めに緩和的な金融環境をなるべく早く脱することが重視される。日銀の基本的なスタンスが、連続利
上げや、0.25％の利上げ幅を上回る大幅利上げは回避するのが望ましいというものであることも踏ま
えると、今後の利上げペースは 3 カ月に一回程度、0.25％ずつが基本になるだろう 1。 
ただし、金融政策の重点が物価上振れ回避にシフトしているなかでは、よりフォワードルッキング
な政策運営が求められる 2。したがって、 原油調達コストの上昇や円安など、 物価上振れリスクの強ま
り次第で利上げペースが速まる可能性がある。先行きの物価を押し上げる主なリスク要因として、日
図表1 9月会合後の総裁会見のポイント 図表2 WTI原油先物

### Page 2

2

銀は、原油高等の中東情勢影響、AI 関連需要の強さ、円安の三点を挙げている。このうち、原油価格
は足元で再び上昇しており、先行きの不確実性も大きい。中東情勢は、物価を取り巻く現下最大のリ
スクだろう。 
■ 不安定な情勢下で再び原油価格が上昇。タンカー運賃も急騰しダブルパンチの様相 
WTI原油先物は、9月に入って一時1バレル=100ドル台と、5月中旬以来の水準に上昇した （図表2） 。
足元では原油価格は幾分低下しているものの、水準としては依然高い状態が続いている。背景には、
中東産原油の供給懸念が強まったことがある。7月下旬以降、 サウジアラビアとイエメン・フーシ派に
よる戦闘が開始し、 ホルムズ海峡を代替する重要な原油輸送ルートとして活用されてきたバブ ・ エル ・
マンデブ海峡の通航リスクが高まった。 また、9月に入り、 サウジアラビアの原油パイプラインが攻撃
を受けて停止したと伝わると、中東産原油の域外輸送が一段と困難になるとの見方が広がった。 
中東情勢が再度悪化するなかで上昇しているのは原油価格だけではない。原油価格の上昇を大幅に
上回る勢いで、原油タンカーの運賃が急騰している（図表3）。8月以降に上昇基調が鮮明となり、9月
中旬には一時、中東情勢悪化前（2026年初）の約46倍、情勢悪化後の3～7月頃と比べても約3.2倍に達
した。 海峡航行リスクが高まるもと、 積み替えや迂回によって原油輸送の効率が悪化しているためだ。
海事データを活用して商品市場を分析しているベルギーの調査会社Kplerによると、 ホルムズ海峡の内
側にあるペルシャ湾から原油を運び出す船と、海峡の外側から最終目的地まで運ぶ船を分け、洋上で
原油を積み替える方式が広がっているようだ。 長距離輸送を担う船が危険な航路を通らずに済む反面、
積み替え作業や待機に時間を要するため、船を次の輸送に回しにくくなる。また、危険な海域を避け
るために遠回りする輸送も増え、船が一度の航海に費やす日数も長くなっている。より多くの輸送資
源が必要となったことで船舶需給がひっ迫し、タンカー運賃が大きく押し上げられている格好だ。

図表3 原油タンカーの運賃 図表4 原油輸入単価と原油価格

### Page 3

■ 今後は日本の原油調達コストが上昇。企業間取引価格は反転加速を予想 
原油価格の上昇に原油タンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの日本の原油輸入単価（単位量あたり
の原油調達コスト）は一段と上昇する見込みだ3。 中東産原油が日本に輸送されるまでには1～2カ月程
度の期間を要するため、8月中旬以降の原油価格上昇 ・原油タンカー運賃上昇は10月以降の原油輸入単
価を押し上げ、その後国内の企業間取引価格へと影響が波及していくと考えられる。足元の原油価格
とタンカー運賃を踏まえると、10月の原油輸入単価は1バレル＝約140ドル程度と、中東情勢悪化後で
最も高い水準に上昇する可能性が高い（図表4）。この影響を輸入全体の平均価格（円建てベース）で
みると、10月の伸びは前年比＋29％程度と、中東情勢悪化後に最も輸入物価の伸びが加速した6・7月
に迫る勢いになる計算だ。さらに、その後は、石油化学製品などの関連財を中心に、国内の企業間取
引価格が上昇しよう。 足元で上昇圧力が一服しつつある国内企業物価は再び加速し、11月頃に同＋7％
台の伸びになると予想する（図表5）。 
加えて、原油輸入金額が膨らんで貿易収支が悪化すれば、円安圧力が強まる可能性がある。原油の
代替調達が進展した6月以降は1兆円に迫る貿易赤字が続いているが、特に10月以降は原油輸入単価が
上昇するもとで本邦貿易赤字が拡大する公算が大きく、実需面からの円安圧力が強まりやすい（図表
6） 。 足元の為替相場は、 米国の利上げ観測を背景に円安に振れやすい地合いだ。 そこに実需の動きが
重なり、1ドル＝160円を上回るようなドル高円安が進行すれば、輸入物価の上振れを通じて、国内企
業物価の伸びが一層高まるリスクがある。 
■ 物価上昇圧力が長期化する場合は利上げペース加速も視野に 
以上のように、当面の企業間取引価格に対しては押し上げ圧力が持続する公算が大きい。物価上昇
圧力が長期化するほど消費者物価への価格転嫁姿勢も強まる可能性があるだけに、日銀は物価上振れ
に対する高い警戒レベルを維持することになろう。 
仮に物価上振れリスクが大きく高まる場合には、 連続利上げ ・大幅利上げの可能性も排除できない。
中東情勢の混乱が長期化し、27年以降も原油輸入単価が一段と上振れることが見込まれる場合、ある

### Page 4

4

いは1ドル=160円を持続的に上回る水準まで円安が進展する場合には 、連続利上げや大幅利上げのシ
ナリオが現実味を帯びてくるだろう。

1 中立金利の水準は不透明であり、利上げの影響はタイムラグをともなって現れる。連続利上げ・大幅利上げによって意図せず
金融引き締めに転じれば、経済へのマイナス影響が必要以上に強まるリスクがある。 
2 中東情勢悪化以降で最初の利上げを決定した 6 月会合を振り返っても、日銀は川上の企業物価の加速を理由の一つとして利上
げに踏み切っている。6 月会合の声明文において日銀は、国内企業物価上昇率が前年比＋6.3％（5 月時点の速報ベース）に加速
したことを踏まえ、 「企業間取引におけるやや速いスピードでの価格転嫁が、消費者段階における幅広い品目の価格上昇に波及
していく可能性がある」と評価した。 
3 中東情勢悪化以降、日本の原油輸入単価は国際市況から大きく上振れている。背景として、主な代替調達先である米国産原油
が需給ひっ迫の懸念等から値上がりし、割高になったことなどが挙げられる。経済産業省発表によると、秋口以降は中東産原油
の調達が増加し、割高な米国産原油に対する依存度は低下すると見込まれるものの、本文記載のように中東産原油の輸送コスト
が大幅に高まっていることから、先行きも割高感の解消は見込みづらい。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

Document 072: 2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2027 年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 
～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 連合は2027 年春闘基本構想で、 定昇込みの賃上げ要求を 「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベア等の
賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とする目安を示した。前年よりも要求姿勢をやや強めて
おり、５％台の賃上げの定着に加え、さらなる引き上げを目指す姿勢がうかがえる。 
○ 経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経労委報告の骨子案では賃上げ
モメンタムの「浸透・定着」が掲げられ、高い賃上げの継続に加え、中小企業への波及を重
視する姿勢が示されている。 
○ 日本経済研究センターのＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査によると、民間エコノミストは 2027 年
春闘の賃上げ率を4.96％と予想（厚生労働省ベース）。前年から小幅に鈍化するものの、高
い伸びが続く見通しであり、４年連続で５％台の賃上げを実現できるかが焦点となる。 
○ 春先にはイラン情勢悪化による賃上げ鈍化が懸念されていたが、現時点で労使双方の姿勢に
トーンダウンはみられない。企業業績の底堅さや構造的な人手不足が高い賃上げを支えてお
り、2027 年春闘の初動のモメンタムは良好と評価できる。

連合は、「６％中心、５％以上」と昨年からトーンをやや強める 
2027 年春闘の集中回答日は2027 年の３月中旬だが、実際の交渉に向けての動きは既に始まりつ
つある。 
本日、連合は「2027 春季生活闘争基本構想」を公表した。春闘に向けて最初に注目される重要
イベントであり、翌年の春闘に向けた労働組合側の基本理念や方針、重点課題を示すものであ
る。この基本構想をみることで、労働側が翌年の春闘にどの程度強気な姿勢で臨もうとしている
かを早い段階で確認することができ、春闘全体のトーンをつかむうえで重要な材料となる。 
今回の基本構想の最大の注目点は、定期昇給相当分を含めて「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベースア
ップ等の賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、より高い賃上げを目指す姿勢を示したことで
ある。「５％以上」という要求水準自体は４年連続（2023 年は「５％程度」）となるが、今回は
「６％中心」との表現が新たに加わった。ベア等の賃上げ分についても、従来の「３％以上」に

### Page 2

2/5 
「４％中心」が加えられている。前年の2026 年春闘では、全体で「５％以上」 、中小で「６％以
上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者では「７％」を目安とする、いわゆる「５・６・７」の要求が
示されていた。今回、全体についても６％を明確に意識する形となったことは、実質的には５％
を最低ラインとしつつ、より高い賃上げを促す姿勢を強めたものと受け取れる。単に高い賃上げ
を続けるだけでなく、その水準をさらに引き上げようとする意欲がうかがえる。

こうした姿勢の背景にあるのが、ここ数年の高い賃上げ実績である。2026 年春闘では連合ベー
スの賃上げ率の実績が5.01％となり、３年連続で５％台の高い賃上げが実現した。５％という水
準がかつてほど実現困難な目標ではなくなりつつあるなか、連合としても前年と同じ要求目安を
繰り返すだけでは、さらなる賃上げを促すメッセージとして十分ではないと判断した可能性があ
る。５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持するだけでなく、６％近辺を意識して賃上げ率のさらなる引
き上げを目指す段階に入ったというメッセージと位置付けられる。 
賃上げを取り巻く環境も、こうした強気の姿勢を支えている。構造的な人手不足が続くなか、
企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き重要な経営課題であり、賃金引き上げの必要性は高
い。また、物価上昇率は2026 年度後半にかけて再び高まるとみられ、家計の生活コスト負担は重
い。名目賃金が増加しても、物価上昇に追いつかなければ生活水準の改善にはつながらない。実
質賃金を安定的にプラスにするためにも、労働側が高い賃上げを求める理由は十分にある。 
加えて、賃上げの広がりという点でも課題は残る。2026 年春闘では中小組合の賃上げ率が
4.69％と高い伸びを確保したものの、全体の5.01％には届かなかった。大企業と中小企業の間に
はなお差があり、正規雇用と非正規雇用の賃金格差も残っている。連合にとっては、高い賃上げ
春闘年 連合の要求目安（定昇相当分込み） 連合ベース 厚労省ベース
2020 ４％程度 1.90％ 2.00％
2021 ４％程度 1.78％ 1.86％
2022 ４％程度 2.07％ 2.20％
2023 ５％程度 3.58％ 3.60％
2024 ５％以上 5.10％ 5.33％
2025 ５％以上 5.25％ 5.52％
2026 ５％以上 5.01％ 5.18％
2027 ６％中心、５％以上 — —
(出所）連合「春季生活闘争基本構想」、連合「春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結果」、厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」
連合の賃上げ要求目安と春闘賃上げ率
実績

### Page 3

3/5 
を実現するだけでなく、それを幅広い企業や労働者に波及させることも引き続き重要な課題であ
る。今回の基本構想でも、賃金実態を把握できないなどの事情がある中小組合については
「20,000 円以上・６％以上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者については「７％」を目安とする方針
が示された。こうした点を踏まえれば、今回の基本構想は、高い賃上げの持続と一段の引き上
げ、そして賃上げの裾野の拡大を目指すものと評価できる。 
また、今回の基本構想では、新たに2030 年の賃金水準を2026 年比で15％程度引き上げる中期
目標も設定された。毎年の賃上げ率だけでなく、中期的に目指す賃金水準を明示することで、高
い賃上げを一過性のものに終わらせず、継続させようとする姿勢がうかがえる。 
なお、近年は個別組合の実際の賃上げ要求率がすでに６％近くに達しており、 「６％中心」とい
う今回の表現には、こうした実態を追認する意味合いもある。今回の基本構想からは、連合の強
い賃上げ意欲が確認できる一方、実際の賃上げ率が前年を上回るかどうかはまた別問題である。
今後は、主要産業別労働組合組織や個別組合の要求水準が前年からどの程度引き上げられるか
（られないか）が注目される。

経営側も賃上げの継続に前向き 
経営側も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経団連が毎年１月に公表する「経
営労働政策特別委員会報告（経労委報告） 」は、春闘に向けた経営側の基本スタンスを示すもので
あり、賃上げ交渉の方向性を占う上で重要な資料となる。2027 年春闘に向けた経労委報告につい
ては、現時点で原案は明らかになっていないものの、９月上旬には骨子案の内容が報道されてい
る。それによると、賃上げモメンタムの「浸透・定着」を掲げ、高い賃上げの継続と中小企業へ
の波及を促す方針とされる。 
経団連は2023 年を賃上げの「起点」 、2024 年を「加速」 、2025 年を「定着」 、2026 年を「さらな
る定着」の年と位置付けてきた。今回の「浸透・定着」という表現からは、高い賃上げを継続す
るとともに、その動きを中小企業などに広げることを重視する姿勢がうかがえる。また、経団連
の筒井会長は９月７日の記者会見で、 「ベースアップ実施の検討を引き続きスタンダードと位置付
けていきたい」と述べ、賃上げのモメンタムを維持する考えを示している。 
数か月前には、イラン情勢の悪化による原材料費や物流費の上昇が企業収益を圧迫し、2027 年
春闘では経営側が賃上げに慎重な姿勢を強めることも懸念されていた。しかし、現時点で経営側
の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられない。 
なお、経労委報告では、原案から正式な報告にかけての修正は例年あまり見られないが、骨子
案から原案にかけては内容が多少修正されることもある。今後、10 月末～11 月中頃までに報道さ
れるとみられる経労委報告の原案が明らかになれば、経営側のスタンスをより具体的に判断でき
るだろう。

エコノミスト予想は4.96％、５％を巡る攻防か 
10 月14 日に日本経済研究センターが公表した「ＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査」では、民間エコノ

### Page 4

4/5 
ミスト 34 人を対象に2027 年春闘の賃上げ率について特別調査を実施している。これによると、
予測値の平均は4.96％となり、2026 年実績の5.18％を 0.22％ポイント下回る見通しとなった。
もっとも、前年から小幅に鈍化するとはいえ、ほぼ５％という歴史的に高い水準であり、2027 年
も高い賃上げが続くとの見方が中心である。なお、ここでの賃上げ率は厚生労働省「民間主要企
業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」ベースであり、連合の集計とは対象が異なる。 
予測の内訳をみると、定期昇給分が1.69％、ベースアップ分が3.27％となっており、ベアにつ
いても３％を超える伸びが見込まれている。また、予測値の高位８機関平均は5.23％、低位８機
関平均は4.68％となっている。予測には一定の幅があるものの、高い賃上げが続くという点では
概ね認識は共有されている。

2027 年春闘で賃上げ率が前年からやや鈍化すると予想される背景には、2026 年の物価上昇率が
2025 年より低く、物価高に対応するための賃上げ圧力が幾分弱まることがあると考えられる。ま
た、数年にわたる高い賃上げによって人件費負担が累積していることに加え、イラン情勢悪化を
背景とした原材料費や物流費の上昇も、企業の賃上げ余力を抑制する要因となり得る。一方、企
業業績が足元まで好調を維持していることや、構造的な人手不足と採用競争の激化は引き続き賃
上げを促す要因であり、賃上げ率が大きく鈍化する可能性も低いとみられる。 
ちなみに、昨年の同時期のＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査でも、2026 年春闘の賃上げ率は4.81％
（2025 年10 月調査）と、前年実績からの鈍化が予想されていた。しかし、実際には5.18％と予
想を上回り、３年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現した。今回も前年からの小幅な鈍化が見込まれて
いるが、予測平均は昨年の同時期をやや上回っている。イラン情勢の悪化という新たな懸念材料
があるなかでも、エコノミストの間では高い賃上げが続くとの見方が維持されていることが分か
（出所）日本経済研究センター「ESPフォーキャスト調査（2026年10月調査）」
（注）2027年は予測値、2026年までの実績は厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
る。 
こうしたなか、予測平均の4.96％について、前年からの「鈍化」を強調するか、5％近傍という
「高水準の持続」を重視するかは、評価が分かれるところだろう。いずれにせよ、2027 年春闘で
は、３年連続で実現した５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持できるか、５％を巡る攻防が一つの焦点
となる。今後、経労委報告原案の判明により経営側の方針が明らかになるほか、主要産業別労働
組合組織の要求方針などが明らかになるにつれて、賃上げ率の見通しもより具体化していくこと
になるだろう。 
なお、この調査とは別に、毎年11 月～12 月にかけて、民間シンクタンク・調査機関から翌年の
春闘賃上げ率の予測が出されることが多い （筆者は 11 月に発表予定） 。 予測をレポートとして作成
することもあれば、 経済見通しレポートのなかで言及されることもあり、春闘賃上げ率の見通しに
ついて、この時期に市場の相場観が徐々に形成されていく。

2027 年春闘の初動は良好 
以上を踏まえると、2027 年春闘の「初動のモメンタム」は、現時点では良好と評価できる。イ
ラン情勢が悪化した数か月前には、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益の圧迫を通じて、
2027 年春闘の賃上げ率が大きく鈍化するとの懸念もあった。しかし、ここまでの動きをみる限
り、労働側、経営側のいずれについても、前年と比べて賃上げ姿勢が慎重化している様子はみら
れない。連合が定昇込み、ベアともに前年より強い要求目安を打ち出し、さらに高い賃上げを志
向する一方、経団連も高い賃上げの継続と中小企業への波及に前向きな姿勢を示している。 
その背景には、原油などの代替調達が進み、資源の調達難が生産活動を大きく制約する事態が
回避されたことや、ＡＩ需要の拡大にも支えられ、企業業績が総じて堅調さを維持していること
があるとみられる。また、構造的な人手不足や採用競争の激化によって、企業が人材確保・定着
のために賃金を引き上げる必要性は依然として高い。物価上昇が続くなか、実質賃金の改善を求
める声が強いことも、高い賃上げに向けての動きを後押ししているとみられる。 
もっとも、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益への悪影響が今後さらに広がる可能性はあ
り、先行きを楽観視することはできない。また、現時点で確認できるのは労使双方の基本的な姿
勢であり、具体的な要求や回答の水準はまだ明らかになっていない。今後は、経団連「経労委報
告」の原案や主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針、早期に賃上げを表明する企業の動向などを通
じて、今回確認された前向きな姿勢が実際の賃上げにつながるかを見極める必要がある。

營收走勢圖

研究員/王建智 MICHAELWANG82121188@UNI-
PSG.COM

研究部對於長榮看法轉趨正向，係因：(1) 3Q26 受惠貨櫃航運
旺季及運價回升，平均運價漲幅明顯高於燃油成本增幅， 帶動獲
利顯著成長。(2) 4Q26 雖進入傳統淡季，惟北美農曆春節前備
貨 、歐洲貨量成長及亞洲區域航線旺季提供支撐，預期獲利雖低
於 3Q26，但仍優於 2Q26。(3) 2027 年雖面臨新船交付增加的供
給壓力， 但全球碼頭擴建速度落後船隊成長，加上塞港及紅海繞
航持續消耗有效運力，有助減緩新增供給對運價的衝擊， 北美及
歐洲航線長約運價亦預期高於 2026 年，支撐全年獲利。預估
2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為45.21 元（+42.7%YoY）／27.89 元
（-38.3%YoY）。歷史本淨比區間為 0.4～1.8 倍，以 2027 年底
預估每股淨值 298.36 元為評價基礎，給予0.95 倍本淨比， 目標
價 283 元，將投資評等調升至買進。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 運價漲幅高於燃油成本增幅，帶動獲利顯著成長 
長榮 3Q26 營收 1,467.76 億元，+39.6%QoQ/+51.4%YoY，主要受
惠第三季貨櫃航運傳統旺季， 歐美航線進入零售商備貨高峰， 帶
動貨運需求回升，加上紅海繞航、 全球港口壅塞持續消耗有效運
力，以及航商透過艙位控管調節供給，推升主要航線運價。7、8
月平均運費分別為 1,539 及1,577 美元／TEU， 參考9 月SCFI 月
均 3,657 點，+8.0%MoM， 推估9 月平均運費約 1,703 美元／TEU，
並假設 9 月貨量與 8 月持平，則 3Q26 平均運費約 1,606 美元／
TEU，+44.7%QoQ，貨量276 萬 TEU，-1.8%QoQ，營收成長主要由
運價上漲貢獻。

成本方面 ， 預估每噸耗油成本由2Q26 的 645 美元升至 714 美元 ，
+10.6%QoQ，假設季度耗油量持平，燃油費用增加約22 億元，惟
運價漲幅明顯高於燃油成本增幅 ， 營收增加帶來的獲利貢獻可望
大幅超過成本上升影響。預估 3Q26 稅後淨利 479.69 億元，
+199.2%QoQ/+120.6%YoY，稅後EPS 22.16 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

圖一、各月平均運費、每噸耗油成本、貨量

資料來源：長榮，統一投顧整理 
2. 4Q26 進入傳統淡季，惟區域航線需求支撐營運 
第四季為貨櫃航運傳統淡季， 整體需求不如第三季旺季強勁， 惟
根據訂艙及裝載情況 ， 至11 月前貨量仍具支撐 。 北美航線方面 ，
美國主要港口進口貨櫃量維持高檔，零售庫存銷售比低於 1.3，
顯示零售商庫存水位相對偏低，仍有持續補貨需求，預期 11 月
貨量隨淡季趨緩 ，12 月則有農曆春節前備貨需求支撐 。 歐洲航線
方面 ，2026 年貨量成長優於預期 ， 雖運價自7 月後逐步回落 ， 但
紅海地緣政治風險尚未解除， 繞航仍持續消耗有效運力。 亞洲區
域航線第四季進入傳統旺季 ， 受惠中國加一帶動東南亞及南亞原
料、半成品運輸需求成長。此外，全球塞港造成約300 萬至 400
萬 TEU 有效運力損失，占全球總運力約 11%至 12%，搭配航商艙
位控管，有助支撐淡季運價。

預估 4Q26 營收 1,174.21 億元，-20.0%QoQ/+37.0%YoY，稅後淨
利 255.68 億元，-46.7%QoQ/+200.2%YoY，稅後 EPS 11.81 元。

3. 2027 年新船交付壓力增加，港口壅塞限制有效運力供給 
2027~2029 年為全球貨櫃船新船交付高峰，目前全球貨櫃船總運
力約3,400 萬TEU，至2030 年待交付新船運力超過1,400 萬TEU，
主流研調機構(Clarksons、Drewry)預估 2027 年名目運力供給增
速為 7.7~7.9%，供給成長壓力仍是 2027 年主要風險。惟名目運
力增加不代表有效運力等幅成長 ， 過去十年全球碼頭處理能力年
均成長約 2.7%，低於船隊運力年均成長約 5.4%，港口基礎設施
擴張速度落後船隊成長，塞港問題短期難以改善。紅海及蘇伊士
運河恢復通行亦為重要變數， 歐洲航線若恢復通行，單程約節省

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

7 天 、 往返約14 天 ， 但船舶集中抵港可能加劇碼頭壅塞 ， 抵銷部
分航程縮短釋出的運力。長榮截至 2030 年尚有 72 艘新船待交
付 ， 合計運力約89 萬 TEU， 並持續投資重要樞紐碼頭 ， 提升靠泊
效率及船隊調度彈性。

4. 2027 年歐美長約運價預期高於 2026 年，支撐全年獲利 
2027 年北美及歐洲航線長約運價預期均高於 2026 年，主要受惠
三項因素 。 首先 ，2026 年第三季貨櫃運價大幅上漲 ， 目前現貨運
價明顯高於前一年度長約議價時的水準 ， 為新年度合約價格調升
奠定基礎 。 其次 ， 全球港口處理能力擴張速度落後船隊運力成長 ，
加上紅海繞航及主要港口壅塞持續消耗有效運力 ， 航商供給調節
能力提升，有利維持長約議價能力。最後，歐美航線貨運需求仍
具支撐 ， 北美零售商庫存水位偏低 ， 歐洲航線貨量成長優於預期 ，
提供長約價格上調條件。歐洲航線新約自 2027 年初開始適用，
北美航線則自 5 月起生效 ， 預期新年度長約運價調升將逐步反映
於營收，並有助減緩現貨運價波動對獲利的影響，支撐 2027 年
營運表現。

5. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 45.21/27.89 元，塞港及長約運
價調升支撐獲利高於歷史平均水準 
預估 2026/2027 年營收分別為 4,558.69／4,178.70 億元，分別
+20.3%YoY/-8.3%YoY，稅後淨利分別為 978.74／603.81 億元，
分別+42.7%YoY/-38.3%YoY，稅後 EPS 分別為 45.21 元／27.89
元。2026 年受惠第三季運價大幅上漲 ， 帶動全年獲利回升 。2027
年雖面臨新船交付增加及運價自高檔回落的壓力 ， 惟全球碼頭處
理能力成長落後船隊運力，加上塞港及紅海繞航限制有效供給，
歐美航線長約價格亦預期高於 2026 年 ， 有助減緩獲利下滑幅度 。

司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資

陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
September Sales Rose 3%
MoM, 27% YoY
Here we focus on Airtac's monthly sales, which we believe could
be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• September sales came in at NT$3,811mn (+3% MoM/+27% YoY).
• 3Q26 revenue was NT$11,512mn (-5% QoQ/+40% YoY), in line with our
estimate of NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41% YoY) but slightly better than
consensus at NT$11,215mn (-8% QoQ/+36% YoY).
• By segment, shipments of machine tool (+35% YoY), energy & lighting (+30%

YoY), electronics (+27% YoY) and textiles (+25% YoY) outperformed during
the month.
Outlook:
• Both order booking and shipments continued to exceed company
expectations in the month.
• Looking ahead, management remains positive on its growth outlook, driven
by share gains and stimulus policies, which could help sustain the upcycle at
least into 2027.
• We remain OW as current valuation at 18x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its
mid-cycle average of 25x, though we think Hiwin (2049.TW) might

outperform on share price on the back of sequential growth in the top line
and potentially GM in 3Q26.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 15 October 2026
Global Research
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing
Full speed on capacity expansion, backed by a
strong US commitment
Cloud AI demand strengthening, with no signs of slowing
TSMC management remains optimistic of a stronger multi-year Cloud AI mega-trend. Its
optimism is based on its prudent and comprehensive assessment of data centre
construction, power shell build up, component supply, and the financial situation of
Cloud service operators. Its capex target was retained at US$60-64bn in 2026, implying

a meaningful uptick to US$19.4-23.4bn in Q4 vs US$40.6bn in 9M26. Our recent
research highlighted TSMC is further accelerating expansion across N3/N2/A14, and
could lift capex to US$90bn in 2027E and US$105bn in 2028E.
Stronger commitment to  US expansion
TSMC guided the overseas dilution on gross margin to rise to 3-4% in 2027 from a
narrowing to 2% (vs. prior 2-3%) in 2026 given the accelerating expansion especially in
the US. The second fab in Arizona should start mass production for N3 in Q327, slightly

earlier than prior plan. Management also stated that it's in talk with SpaceX/Terafab on
potential collaboration, with key principle being to operate the fabs by TSMC.  As what
we wrote recently, TSMC is also evaluating the possibility to expand outside  Arizona,
with Texas being an option. The greater ambition to expand global footprint and
support reshoring is key in solidifying TSMC's competitive positioning, in our view.
Better Q4 sales guidance, slightly down GM guidance not concerning

Q3 EPS of NT$29.43 was above LSEG consensus of NT$28.67, mostly due to higher sales
and OPM of 58.7%. Q3 gross margin of 66.4% was a bit below expectation of 67%
into earnings, given N2 dilution. Q4 sales is guided to grow 14% QoQ at mid point,
slightly ahead of expectation of ~10% QoQ into earnings, driving full year sales
guidance in USD to grow 44% YoY. TSMC guided greater dilution from N2 and overseas
expansion, which lowers Q4 GM 40bps QoQ to 66.0% at the mid point of guidance. We

view TSMC’s investment case on strong growth at sustainable margins with good
support in 2027  (UBS GMs up 10bps YoY at 66.7% from 66.6% in 2026) with price
hikes to pass on higher costs.
Valuation: maintain Buy and target of NT$3,650
We raise 2026-28E EPS by 2-3% to reflect higher sales even with slightly lower gross
margin assumptions. Our new 2028E EPS is 10% above consensus pre-earnings. We
view resilient risk/reward for TSMC stock at 16x 2027E PE vs 36% earnings growth in

2027 based on UBSe, and expect the stock to steadily outperform the broader tech
sector at least through early 2027, assuming the market volatility persists. Reiterate Buy
rating. Our price target is based on 20x 2027-28E PE with a 26% long-term earnings
CAGR.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation 49.6%

Forecast stock return 50.9%

Forecast excess return 44.5%
Company Description
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (TSMC) is the world's leading dedicated foundry
service provider, with annual capacity of about 32m of 8-inch equivalent wafers as of end-
2024. It operates four 12-inch wafer fabs, four 8-inch wafer fabs and one 6-inch wafer fab in

Taiwan. TSMC also manages two 8-inch fabs and one 12-inch fab overseas under wholly
owned subsidiaries WaferTech in the US, and TSMC China and TSMC Nanjing in China.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for TSMC on a PE multiple.
We think TSMC faces a variety of risks, including rapidly changing technology, intense
competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand. TSMC also has a history of paying
stock options to its employees, which could dilute earnings.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67 元 
3Q26進入旺季，營收季增 3%至 496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1
百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3百分點及記憶體持
續上漲。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7元，比原估下
修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%。4Q26 NCR代工訂單/帆宣美國二廠持
續增加，估營收季增 3%，毛利率季持平，稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，
EPS 9.1元，比原估上修 8%，主因 Kontron持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
樺漢提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交
割完成 )。影響： 1)財務面：本中心 預期提高持股後， 估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。2)軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、
交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電
腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
上修 2026/27 年獲利 1%/6% 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578 億元，MoM+17%，未來成長趨
勢明確。展望 2026~27 年主要工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄
廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，看好新廠商機，包括設備代
工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。由於認列美國二廠業績加快，
估 2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
合計樺漢 2026/27 年營收 1,864/2,104 億元，成長 31%/13%，規模經濟向
上，費用率下滑，估營業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，優於 2025年 3.99%。稅後
44.4/53.6 億元，年增 38%/21%，EPS 2 8.25/34.14 元，比先前預期上修
1%/6%，主因升高子公司 Kontron持股。樺漢受惠提升 Kontron持股及帆宣
認列美國二廠增加，2026/27年營收及獲利高成長，維持買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
估 4Q26 稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，年增 64%，EPS 9. 1 元，比原估
上修 8%，主因 Kontron 持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
⧫  
樺漢將 Kontron  持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%，估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。並從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
⧫  
受惠提升 Kontron 持股及帆宣認列美國二廠增加 ，營收及獲利高成
長，維持買進。目標價 545 元係以 2027 年 EPS、16 倍本益比推得。

營運分析  
估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7 元，比原估下修 5%，
主因營收低於原先預期 4% 
3Q26 進入旺季，NCR 代工訂單持續拉貨，子公司帆宣美國二廠進入認列高峰期，營收季增 3%至
496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3
百分點及記憶體持續上漲。估營業利益 28.3億元，季增 5%，年增 504%。業外方面，投資收入及
其他收益挹注下，共獲利 5.8億元。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67元(以最新
稀釋後股本 15.7 億元計算)，比原估下修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%(智慧軟體方案部門需求
疲弱)。

4Q26進入最旺季，NCR代工訂單/子公司帆宣美國二廠持續增加，估營收季增 3%至 513億元，年

入及其他收益挹注下，共獲利 2.4億元。估稅後 14.3億元，季增 19%，年增 64%，EPS 9.1元(以
最新稀釋後股本 15.7億元計算)，比原估上修 8%，主因 Kontron持股由 29.7%上升至 49.4%，少
數股權項目扣除降低。

記憶體漲價大部分轉嫁給客戶，略影響毛利率，但規模經濟向上，營業利益率上揚 
關於記憶體漲價因應，公司已與供應商鎖定全年約 200億元訂單所需之記憶體供貨量，價格雖隨市
場浮動，但會透過長約定價或調價等轉嫁給客戶，若記憶體價格漲幅過大也採客戶 consign 模式，
降低毛利率影響。1Q26 部分客戶接受轉嫁，部分客戶則選擇先觀望遞延專案，造成營收不如預期。
但 2Q26 後客戶已逐漸接受記憶體漲價，出貨恢復正常。由於記憶體轉嫁有落後且難以全數移轉，
加上近期低毛利率的子公司帆宣廠務成長較快，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 19.4%/19.3%，略低於
2025年 20.5%。但預估 2026/27年整體營收成長 31%/13%，規模經濟明顯向上，費用率下滑，營
業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，遠優於 2025年 3.99%。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第4 頁，共 19頁  
積極佈局實體 AI 
公司將實體 AI(Physical AI)定義為 AI由數位分析與內容生成，進一步具備感知、理解、決策及實際
執行能力。樺漢過去由 IPC 1.0 工業電腦、IPC 2.0 工業物聯網及 IPC 3.0 智慧平台逐步升級，
2026年開始進一步布局 Physical AI。 
公司建立完整機器人系統工程團隊，涵蓋 AI 感知與理解、機器學習系統、機器人運動控制、嵌入
式運算平台、數位孿生模擬及可靠度設計。 AI 感知包含影像、語音、感測器融合及 VLA（Vision-
Language-Action）技術；模擬驗證採用 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 進行虛擬場景、產線及機器人流程模擬
測試，用以最佳化生產效率。樺漢競爭策略由單純提供板卡與運算模組，轉向提供完整機器人系統
平台。產品布局涵蓋 Sense、Compute、Connect、Control及 Act五大機器人平台零件，應用包括
AMR自主移動機器人、人形機器人、工業製造、智慧物流及商業服務。  
運動控制及機器人相關技術仍在持續投資；模擬驗證軟體毛利率可超過 60%。管理層認為 Physical 
AI 初期仍以研發與平台建置為主，短期商業化重點為工業及商用 AI 應用，人形機器人獲利貢獻仍
需時間。2026年實體 AI為初期發展階段，貢獻樺漢業績約 3億美金，占營收比重 5.2%。長期實
體 AI 於製造場域可協助採購與物流管理、改善長短料問題，並透過即時感測與自動化控制提升工
站品質、精度及生產效率，研調機構預估全球實體 AI產值由 2025年 58億美金，上升至 2035元
產值可達 1200 億美元， CAGR 35.4%。樺漢結合既有智慧工廠與工業物聯網客戶基礎，推動
Physical AI走向實際部署，可望快速成長。

圖 8：樺漢機器人與智慧餐飲佈局

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
因樺漢集團對 Kontron的總持股比例已來到約 29.72%，接近德國證券法規定的 30%強制公開收購
門檻，樺漢董事會正式授權董事長啟動現金公開收購之前置作業(訂定每股 23.5歐元)。樺漢公開收
購並提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交割完成)，核心效益與影
響主要體現在以下幾個面向： 
1)財務面：本中心預期提高持股後，估計可望提升獲利 10%以上(少數股權項目扣除減少)，雙方深
化整合後，透過樺漢母公司鴻海製造資源，預期從 2027 年開始全面發酵具體的營收與成本規模經
濟效益。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第6 頁，共 19頁  
2)業務與技術整合(實體 AI 平台化) 
• 軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢
在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解
決方案」。 
• 加速跨場景落地：有助於將無人結帳、智慧零售、 AMR（自主移動機器人）及工廠自動化等應用
標準化，降低客戶導入門檻，並加快在北美及歐洲市場的開案與交付效率。  
Kontron 成長領域包括交通運輸、國防航太、 5G/FRMCS/NAD，以及軟體與解決方案，營收目標
由 2026年 16億歐元上升至 2030年 26億歐元(CAGR 13%)、EBITDA達 4.2億歐元，並藉由提高
平台及服務收入占比改善獲利結構。

結盟 NCR 增加 ODM 訂單 2Q26 開始出貨，有助工業物聯網部門成長 
樺漢與 NCR Voyix (股票代號：VYX US)結盟，該公司是全球消費者技術的領導者，主要生產 POS、
自動櫃員機、條碼閱讀器等，提供高品質的技術解決方案，並幫助企業在快速變化的數位環境中 蓬
勃發展。NCR Voyix 在市場領先的軟體和服務與樺漢的硬體專業知識技術高度互補，並有助樺漢
ESaaS及 ODM業務進一步擴展，為雙贏的結盟。 
NCR年營收約 1,230億元，其中硬體占比 32%，軟體 68%，硬體部分約 400億元台幣訂單未來全
交給樺漢代工，其中 160億元為新接訂單[NCR之前即為樺漢 ODM客戶，60%硬體(約 240億元)
交樺漢生產]，此部分將貢獻在工業物聯網 (含 Kontron、ODM)業務上，2025 年因應川普關稅議題，
NCR IT 全球性服務需調整期，發展整合系統；樺漢墨西哥製造基地調整至田納西。因此原本增加
NCR代工訂單由 10月再度延至 2026年 1月出貨，後因美伊戰爭及 DRAM漲價影響，客戶延至
2Q26正式出貨。受惠此訂單增加，估 2026/27 年工業物聯網營收成長 24%/13%至 845/958億元。

智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)進入美國二廠密集認列期，營運創新高 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578億元，MoM+17%，主要來自晶圓代工廠、記憶體廠擴
廠積極，另外新增資料中心建置，半導體供應鏈普遍面臨交期、產能壓力，加上具有能力承接大型
專案廠商有限，使得廠務接近賣方市場，未來 2~3 年成長趨勢更為明確。展望 2026~27 年主要
工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，並且看
好新廠商機，包括設備代工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣) 認
列美國二廠業績加快，2026 年前 9 月營收成長 56%至 574 億元，第四季旺季營收持續向上，估
2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
2026/27 年年營收及獲利維持高成長 
智慧軟體方案策略進入 IPC 3.0Ⅱ階段，推廣工控/智慧平台，未來專注於雲端+邊緣 AI與大數據外，
亦提升工業級語言模型與機器人技術，聚焦產業包括：國防、航太、交通、零售、建築等。產品包
括：Edge AI Server & Device、Token、AI Agent、Physical AI等，公司積極佈局中，近期因專案
認列延後，估 2026 年營收 218 億元，衰退 3%。2027 年代理/實體 AI 發酵，估營收成長 7%至
233億元。 
合計樺漢 2026/27年營收 1,864/2,104億元(下修 2%/2%，主因智慧軟體方案營收低於預期)，成長
31%/13%，低毛利率的智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)成長較大及上游原物料 Dram大漲，估 2026/27年毛

產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023年產值約為 45億美元，可望增加至 2037年的 96億美元，CAGR 6%比一般 PC低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 9：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20世紀末進入 21世紀，工業製造由 3.0發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

圖 10：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

公司簡介  
樺漢專精於工業電腦硬體解決方案。終端應用包括 POS機、銀行自動化、互動式資訊服務系統、彩券機及工業自動化。產
品組合方面(2025)，工業物聯網 48%、智慧工廠與廠務 36%、智慧軟體與方案 16%。銷售區域 (2025)北美 26%、歐洲
37%、中國 9%、其他 28%。主要生產交由母公司鴻海代工，另外樺漢在歐洲新設匈牙利自有工廠，取得當地娛樂、金融
零售、工業網安等訂單，維繫 ODM業務成長。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 樺漢整體的 ESG風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名略為領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 樺漢的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略高於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含公司資安、商業道德、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：樺漢已採取若干措施來管理與重大 ESG議題相關的風險，但這些措施尚不足以涵蓋
其所面臨的所有風險。儘管如此，該公司並未捲入具有重大財務影響的風險事件。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

個股聚焦  
2026/10/20

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

연도별 CMO 수주금액 현황

자료: 삼성바이오로직스, SK증권

Document 238: AI行情震荡，去香港金融科技周听听掌舵者怎么说

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI行情震荡，去香港金融科技周听听掌舵者怎么说.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

华尔街⻅闻 2026 ⾹港⾦融科技周深度调研
摘要：
AI投资这笔账，⾦融巨头怎么看？
营收和利润都超出预期，股价却跌了近 7% 。
10 ⽉ 22 ⽇， Meta 财报后的市场反应，再次显露出投资者对 AI 投⼊的担忧。持续增加的⽀出、庞
⼤的融资需求，让市场重新审视这场投资竞赛。
巨额投⼊还在继续，资⾦能否持续跟上，回报⼜将在什么时候出现？
研究这笔账，需要把技术进展与商业需求放在⼀起看。科技公司准备投⼊多少，⾦融机构怎样
评估⻛险，客⼾愿意为哪些应⽤付费，共同影响着这轮产业扩张能⾛多远。
在 10 天后开幕的⾹港⾦融科技周上，有两场讨论值得带着这些疑问去听。
⼀场追问 “AI 变便宜后，谁能盈利？ ” 耐能创始⼈兼 CEO 刘峻诚、汇丰集团创投负责⼈ Moran
Levinovitz 等，将探讨 AI 成本下降后，产业链上的利润如何分配。
另⼀场聚焦⾦融 AI 的规模化应⽤与商业化。⾹港投资推⼴署助理署⻓梁瀚璟、⻨肯锡⾹港管理
合伙⼈ Arthur Shek 等，将讨论 AI 如何转化为业务收益，以及应⽤中的责任边界。

11 ⽉ 2 ⽇⾄ 3 ⽇，⼤会主论坛将在⾹港会议展览中⼼举⾏。今年的嘉宾还包括⾹港特区⾏政⻓官
李家超、财政司司⻓陈茂波、⾹港⾦管局总裁余伟⽂，以及汇丰、渣打集团 CEO 等。政策制定
者、⾦融机构负责⼈和科技企业经营者的讨论，为投资者提供了相互参照的视⻆。
华尔街⻅闻此次的港深调研，也将沿着这些问题展开：11⽉ 1 ⽇⾄ 5 ⽇，从两天⼤会，到⾹港⾦
融机构交流，再到深圳企业参访，把会场⾥的观点与具体业务放在⼀起看。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

听政策⽅向：看清创新进⼊市场的条件
⾹港⾦融科技周曾是重要政策的发布现场。 2025 年，⾦管局在会上公布 “ ⾦融科技 2030” ，将数
据与⽀付基础设施、⾦融机构应⽤ AI 等列为重点⽅向。
因此，今年到现场听监管者的讨论，可以继续关注：哪些⽅向获得⽀持，推进需要哪些配套，
机构还⾯临什么实际障碍。对研究科技与⾦融⾏业的⼈来说，这些信息有助于理解⼀项创新从
技术⽅案进⼊商业应⽤的过程。
听机构判断：了解资本与业务需求如何变化
今年⼤会的三⼤引擎是⾦融 + 、 AI+ 、出海。议程还安排了全球资本投向、 AI 与财富管理，以及
企业全球化与跨境⽀付等讨论。
同⼀项技术，在不同参与者眼中可能有不同的价值。科技公司强调产品能⼒，银⾏关注可靠性
与业务效率，资管机构关⼼它将怎样影响企业的竞争⼒与盈利。

### Page 3

把这些判断对照着听，可以进⼀步厘清：哪些需求正在形成，哪些应⽤仍有障碍，市场期待与
企业实际进展之间还有多⼤距离。
⾹港连接内地产业与国际⾦融市场，也让这些讨论具有更⼴的参照范围。关注科技投资，可以
同时听到资本与客⼾的声⾳；关注跨境配置，也可以了解⾦融服务正在发⽣哪些变化。
看企业实践：把会场⾥的问题带到业务现场
两天⼤会期间，除了听论坛，还可以到展区了解产品：⾯向什么客⼾，解决什么问题，实际使
⽤需要具备哪些条件。具体的产品演⽰，可以帮助理解演讲中提到的技术与业务模式。
本次参会还将设有华尔街⻅闻专属报告厅，安排嘉宾到厅分享。
⼤会之后，我们将继续⾛访⾹港⾦融机构与深圳企业。
11 ⽉ 4 ⽇，在某国际⼤⾏，听取专业⼈⼠对全球资产配置、 AI 产业变化与中美权益市场的观察；
在拔萃资本，交流家族及⾼净值客⼾的财富管理；通过银乾国际与某国际保险机构的分享，进
⼀步了解⾹港资管业务及保险产品的运⾏机制。
这些交流，将⼤会中的⾏业议题延伸到投资者关⼼的配置思路、财富管理服务与具体业务。当
晚的跑⻢地赛⻢及晚餐，也为同⾏读者留出交流⻅闻的时间。

11 ⽉ 5 ⽇，调研将⾛进深圳企业，参访⽅向涵盖⾦融、互联⽹、 AI 、⾃动驾驶与机器⼈。通过⾯
对⾯交流，了解企业怎样开发产品、服务客⼾，以及技术在实际业务中⾯临哪些要求。
具体路线⻅上⽅⽇程图。每位参与者将随机分配⾄其中⼀条路线，参访三家企业，暂不⽀持⾃
选路线，具体以最终安排为准。
从市场波动中产⽣的问题，可以带到⼤会⾥听取不同判断，再到机构和企业中寻找更具体的参
照。这也是我们将⼤会、机构交流与企业参访安排在同⼀趟⾏程中的⽤意。
如果您也关注科技投资、全球市场与跨境资产配置，欢迎带着正在研究的问题，与华尔街⻅闻
⼀起出发。
11⽉ 1 ⽇⾄ 5 ⽇，⾹港 · 深圳，限额 25 ⼈。
扫码添加 Alpha ⼩助⼿，咨询参会安排、调研权益及报名详情。

### Page 8

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

2026 年三季度以来，全球市场巨震：

10 ⽉ 25 ⽇周⽇晚上 19 点，百亿私募基⾦敦和资管的⾸席经济学家徐⼩庆主讲的Alpha 线上闭⻔私
享会，将会分享他最近研究关于股票、债券、商品、外汇、⻩⾦等⼤类资产的最新变化，研判
当前宏观⾛势与 2026 下半资产配置⽅向，看清背后的配置逻辑与关键时机，助⼒个⼈的关键决
策。
这是徐⼩庆今年第三次做客 Alpha 闭⻔会。

Document 291: Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

13023135     1

Economy 
Entering the age of the older consumer 
01 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• The older consumer is moving center stage. America's population has been aging for decades, but the shift has accelerated: the 
share aged over 60 increased by almost 10 percentage points (pp) between 1995 and 2025. And the Census Bureau projects the 
share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population. 
• More free time means a different spending mix. People aged 65 and over spend around two and a half fewer hours per day 
working and roughly two hours more on leisure than the overall adult population. Bank of America data shows that older 
households allocate relatively more of their card spending to groceries and travel, and less to restaurants, gasoline, general 
merchandise and clothing. 
• Households aged over 55 held close to $140 trillion in net worth in the second quarter of 2026, giving many older consumers 
considerable capacity to spend on travel and other leisure categories. But wealth is not evenly distributed, and healthcare and 
other cost pressures remain significant for older households on tighter or fixed incomes.

Population aging is nothing new, but it is accelerating  
The US population has been growing relatively “older” for a long time. Exhibit 1 shows that the proportion of the population 
under 20 years old shrank from the mid-1960s through 2025. At the same time, the proportion of the population in older age 
groups has been rising.  
Exhibit 1: Over time the US population has steadily aged  
Proportion of US population by age range (%)

Source: Human Mortality Database; Bank of America Institute calculations

Additionally, there has been a notable acceleration in the relative aging of the population in recent years. For example, between 
1995 and 2025, the proportion of the population aged over 60 rose by almost 10 percentage points (pp) to 25%.  
Several factors have driven this. First, fertility rates in the US, as in much of the world, have fallen sharply. This has slowed 
natural population growth and, by reducing the number of younger people relative to older cohorts, has increased the share of 
the population at older ages. Second, more recently, net inward migration to the US has slowed. Because migrants tend to be 
younger than the overall population, weaker immigration also tends to raise the average age. Finally, rising life expectancy over 
much of the post-war period has allowed more people to survive into older age, further increasing the older population's share.  
Exhibit 2: The trend towards an older composition of the population is expected to continue  
Forecast changes in the share of population by age group relative to 2025 (pp)

Source: United States Census Bureau 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Lower fertility and, more recently, weaker net migration look set to slow overall US population growth in the decades ahead. As a 
result, older Americans are likely to play an increasingly important role in the economy, both as a share of the population and, in 
many age groups, in absolute numbers. The Census Bureau projects the share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 
2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population (Exhibit 2). For businesses, understanding how the needs and spending patterns 
of older consumers differ from those of younger generations will therefore become increasingly relevant. 
Older age groups allocate their time differently  
Why does this matter? One reason is that older people tend to spend their time and money quite differently from younger adults. 
Most obviously, many work less or have retired altogether. Data from the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) shows that people 
aged 65 and over spend, on average, around two and a half fewer hours per day working than the population aged 15+ (Exhibit 
3). 
That leaves considerably more time for other activities. People aged 65 and over spend around two hours more per day on 
leisure and sports activities, with television viewing accounting for a particularly large share of this additional leisure time 
(Exhibit 4).

Older households also spend differently  
Bank of America internal data on credit and debit card spending provides insights into how these differences in older 
generations’ use of time also impact their spending. Rather than splitting our spending data by generation, as we often do, here 
we split by age bands, allowing for consistent comparisons over time.  
Exhibit 5 shows how the composition of card spending of households aged 61-75 and those older than 75 differs from that of all 
households. A few things stand out. For one, older households devote a greater share of their overall card spending to groceries 
(food and beverages). Households aged 61-75 also allocate more spending to travel (airlines and lodging). And restaurants and 
bars, gasoline, general merchandise and clothing account for a smaller share of older households’ card spending.  
These differences may seem fairly intuitive. Older households have more leisure hours, so spend relatively more on travel to 
enjoy them. Previously we found that more available time may support travel particularly around retirement, although the 
discretionary spending lift can be temporary (for more, read our publication: Paychecks to pensions: The evolution of retiree 
spending).  
Older households are also likely to have less need to update their work wardrobes, so they probably spend less on clothing.

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Still, older households’ relatively lower spending on restaurants and bars is a little more curious, perhaps, given dining out could 
be one way of making use of more leisure time. Indeed, when we look at how these spending shares are evolving, we find a 
nuanced picture. While households aged 61-75 do spend relatively less on restaurants and bars than the overall population, they 
appear to be narrowing this gap. But for those older than 75 this is not the case. In our view, the pandemic may still be weighing 
on dining-out spending among this older age group (Exhibit 6).  
Exhibit 6: For restaurants and bars the story is mixed across older 
cohorts…

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Interestingly, however, the airline spending share of both older groups has recovered more strongly since the pandemic than that 
of all households, although it remains below its 2019 level (Exhibit 7).

Aging presents opportunities, but also challenges 
In our view, the aging of the US population presents important opportunities for the economy and businesses. Older consumers 
have more leisure time and businesses that cater to these demands may therefore benefit. 
Moreover, some older consumers hold considerable amounts of net wealth and have been some of the biggest beneficiaries of 
recent rises in equities. Using data from the Federal Reserve, Exhibit 8 shows that as of the second quarter of 2026 the net 
worth of those households aged 55 and over was close to $140 trillion, around three-quarters of the total. And with equity 
markets rising strongly, these age groups have seen a more than 20% increase in their net worth over the past two years, 
potentially enhancing their spending power further.  
But an important caveat is that not all older consumers are in this position. Previous Institute work found evidence consistent 
with equity-market wealth effects being concentrated toward higher-income households (read our publication: Paychecks to 
pensions: The evolution of retiree spending). And Census Bureau research has found that around 14% of Social Security 
recipients aged over 65 rely on it for over 90% of their income. For older households without a wealth cushion, recent elevated 
cost pressures, like gasoline, will likely have put them under more significant financial strain. They will also be more dependent 
on cost-of-living (COLA) increases in Social Security to underpin their spending.  
And, more broadly, outside of card spending, healthcare costs are a much larger factor in older households’ budgets (Exhibit 9). 
The evolution of these costs going forward will be a key variable for older consumers’ budgets.  
Exhibit 8: A large and growing proportion of net worth is held by

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts.  
Durables spending is defined as spending on electronics, building materials, auto-related spending and furniture. Premium durables spending is 
based on a selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively higher value products. Conversely, value durables spending is based on a 
selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively lower value products.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Following on?
• We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps
to 3% at the 28 October Review.
• A vote seems very possible as differences in
view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook
has really shifted in recent weeks.
• Data since September show a higher outlook
for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP
starting point, but increased risks to domestic
consumption and residential investment. On

balance, the medium-term inflation picture
will probably be judged to have worsened
slightly given recent movements in energy
prices and the exchange rate.
• Forward guidance will likely leave open
the option to raise the OCR further at the
December Monetary Policy Statement without
presenting it as a sure thing.
• Given the ‘pause’ signal in September, the
press release will likely emphasise the more
concerning short-term inflation picture driven

by the lower exchange rate and higher energy
prices. The tightening in financial conditions
through higher short- and long-term interest
rates, as well as lingering spare capacity, will
be presented as offsets that means the MPC
retains confidence that inflation will return
towards 2% over time.
• We still think a more measured tightening
approach is appropriate from here. Raising the
OCR at every meeting until something ‘breaks’
could be a risky strategy if downside growth

concerns crystalise.
• Nonetheless, the expected hike will bring the
OCR to the top of the RBNZ’s neutral range
more quickly. We now think the OCR will hit
3.5% a meeting earlier in February. Thereafter
we expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, lifting the OCR to a
peak of 4%.

RBNZ decision and communication.
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. As there will likely be differences
in perspective about recent developments in energy
prices and the exchange rate, we think it is quite possible
that the decision will be reached after a formal vote,
rather than by consensus.
As the RBNZ will not be presenting new forecasts (aside

perhaps from updated short-term CPI forecasts) we will
not see an updated OCR forecast track. Rather we expect
the RBNZ will aim to leave their options open at the next
meeting in December. As in September, we expect the
RBNZ to note that conditional on the central economic

ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of RBNZ October 2026
Monetary Policy Review

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
outlook, the OCR may need to increase further (the
September projection had portrayed an OCR eventually
rising to 3.25%). The RBNZ will emphasise the future OCR
path is not predetermined.
In September, the MPC agreed that further tightening in
policy was likely required. The bulk of the MPC saw upside
risks to the inflation outlook while two members (Conway
and Hansen) thought the risks were balanced. Recent
developments will likely not have made those seeing

upside risks to inflation more comfortable, while the
more dovish MPC members will likely still see the growth
downside risks given continued softness in house prices
and the mounting pressure on real incomes. Indeed, all
MPC members saw some degree of downside risks to
growth which likely remain relevant. All these messages
should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC will likely continue to present their decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July, where

a higher OCR now is insurance against a much higher OCR
later should upside medium-term inflation risks arise. We
doubt the objective will be to push market interest rates
materially higher.
The exchange rate will likely have greater prominence
at this Review, as it did at the July meeting. The TWI is
just over 3% lower than previously assumed which – if
sustained – will add some inflation pressure over the
next few quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted

the RBNZ’s medium term outlook for inflation. So, the
discussion will seek to link the implications of the lower
exchange rate for short term inflation projections with the
risk this spills over into medium term inflation. We doubt
the RBNZ will be trying to actively support the exchange
rate in any meaningful way, and indeed the lower
exchange rate may be presented as something supportive
of the medium-term growth profile, reducing risks there.
Financial conditions more broadly are likely to receive

some attention. While the exchange rate is lower than
assumed, there have also been marked increases in
wholesale long-term interest rates and (to a lesser extent)
mortgage rates. The impact of higher interest rates on
managing medium term inflation risks will likely be noted.
The RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that at this
stage there is little evidence of second-round inflation
impacts coming from the Iran war and the associated rise
in energy prices. We note that most other central banks

are drawing a similar judgement at this point. Today’s CPI
might be consistent with that judgement, although core
inflation measures have increased and will likely be noted
by the more hawkish members of the MPC. We expect the
RBNZ to point to the risks that such persistent inflation
pressures might emerge at some point. But that would
depend on the duration of the oil price shock. We expect
the RBNZ to continue to have the assumption that the
shock dissipates and that energy prices and hence CPI

inflation will be appreciably lower in the medium term.

RBNZ target range
We expect the RBNZ to note the labour market remains
cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Still-weak employment growth compared to labour force

growth, weak job vacancies growth and low levels of
labour market turnover are in line with this view and could
be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge a still-solid
global growth outlook but with significant downside risks
looking forward given the Iran war and concerns around
the sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Implications for Westpac’s forecast profile
for the OCR.
The expected hike will bring the OCR to the top of the

range of the RBNZ’s neutral rate estimates more quickly.
It seems likely that the OCR will hit 3.5% a meeting earlier
now. Beyond the pause that we had previously expected
at next week’s meeting, we had expected the RBNZ would
be looking to slow the pace of OCR increases once it had
reached 3.5%. We retain the latter expectation, so this
earlier hike also implies that the pause will come a little
earlier, after two further 25bp hikes at the December

2026 and February 2027 reviews.
We continue to see further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings – taking the OCR to a peak
of 4.0% – as it’s likely that more restrictive conditions
will be required to push core inflation back sustainably
to 2%. Needless to say, the path of the OCR in 2027 will
depend on the evolution of numerous factors that remain
very uncertain, not least being developments in the
Middle East.
Arguments in favour of a hike.

• The ongoing conflict in the Middle East and related
developments in global energy markets point to upside
risk for headline inflation, at least in the near term.
Westpac’s forecasts for headline inflation are now
at 2.5% or above for most of 2027 now. Upside risks
to core inflation and inflation expectations also may
emerge the longer the oil price remains above $90-
100/bbl.

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• The exchange rate is notably weaker in recent months
as global interest rates have been rising, and the NZ
OCR is low compared to Australian and US settings.
Financial markets even envisage further interest rate
increases in Europe given the high levels of inflation
implied by their September adverse scenarios (3.2%
2027 HICP inflation vs 2.5% baseline). If the NZ OCR
doesn’t keep pace with global trends, then the

risk is for more inflation generated by a lower NZD
exchange rate.
• The ‘real’ OCR rate is relatively low – plausibly around
-1 to -2 % based on headline inflation projections.
Based on core CPI levels the real policy rate is likely
negative. An OCR in the 3.25-3.5% range would deliver
positive real rates based on core CPI forecasts.
• The economy has grown above trend in the last year
reflecting strong export sector incomes and the
lagged impact of past low OCR settings. The output

gap is likely still negative but is closing. Relying on a
prolonged negative output gap to bring core inflation
pressures down could be risky.
• The areas of economic strength in the last year are not
yet waning. Agriculture sector returns remain strong,
while global growth forecasts are being revised up as
the AI investment boom proceeds. The construction
sector is picking up, as is the manufacturing sector.
Tourism is strong, supported by an advantageous

exchange rate.
• Market pricing leans strongly towards an OCR hike at
this meeting. It may be less disruptive to go along with
market pricing, especially given the RBNZ’s forecasts
imply two further OCR increases including one by
year-end. Moving now versus six weeks later is of little
consequence from a macro view. A smaller ‘splash’ at
this meeting could be desirable given the proximity to
the election.
Arguments in favour of no change.
• For all the upward pressure on short term inflation,

there is little evidence that the medium-term inflation
outlook has shifted much. Domestic inflation in the
September quarter was 0.3ppts weaker than the RBNZ
had expected, which should be a source of comfort.
In addition, energy and exchange rate inflation
impacts on inflation occur too early for the MPC to
offset and will be what they will be. And measures of
pricing behaviour and inflation expectations in recent
consumer and business surveys seem benign given the

scale of recent energy price increases.
• Firms report pressure on margins and an inability
to set prices high enough to recoup cost increases.
Profitability is struggling which clouds the outlook.
Firms expect better times ahead – perhaps because
they think energy prices will fall – but note tougher
trading conditions now. Hiring still seems modest
albeit positive. It does not appear that hiring is yet
strong enough to reduce the high unemployment rate.

• Housing market trends look quite weak and seem
set to constrain consumer spending and residential
investment. Despite very low mortgage rates in the
last year, house sales have declined and house prices
are flat to falling. It seems unlikely we will see a change
in these weak trends soon – especially if interest
rates continue to rise. The result is we are not seeing
a ‘consumption rich’ recovery and growth is relatively
unbalanced. There could be additional risks to the

sustainability of this recovery should external demand
suddenly weaken for some reason.
How might the election play into the
decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not
be politically motivated. However, the election outcome
is close. And should it result in a Labour Party-led
coalition, it seems likely the new government would adopt
the Labour Party’s promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual

mandate. That would require the MPC to weigh the degree
of labour market slack more highly in its deliberations. As
the degree of labour market slack currently seems high,
a new mandate might justify the MPC taking more time
to return inflation to the middle of the target range (our
earlier note discusses this issue). This could figure into
the deliberations of some MPC members if the decision
between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it now is

viewed as finely balanced.
Scenarios.
Our core scenario, with perhaps around a 60%
probability, is that the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp
and signals the December meeting is live but without
committing to a markedly tighter future stance than
presented in September. Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the OCR increase to 3% and have the RBNZ more
strongly emphasise the upside risks to core and

medium-term inflation, leading to the implication that
an upward revision of the RBNZ’s forward OCR profile
is in the offing in December. The downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) is one
where the RBNZ leaves the OCR unchanged and signals
that the medium-term outlook for inflation has not
yet changed meaningfully relative to that forecast
in the September meeting, despite the higher near-

term inflation outlook. This would cause the RBNZ to
reiterate its previous forecast, i.e. one 25bp increase in
December and one more hike in 2027 sometime.

### Page 4

4
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Appendix: Key developments since the
September Monetary Policy Statement.
Activity: The economy is coming from a stronger starting
point than the RBNZ assumed in September. GDP rose
by 0.2% in the June quarter, and along with revisions to
earlier quarters, the annual growth rate lifted to 2.6%
against the RBNZ’s forecast of 2.1%. Against this, recent
high-frequency data and the resurgence in fuel prices
presents some downside risk to the RBNZ’s growth

forecasts for the second half of the year. The RBNZ’s
Nowcast estimates of growth have drifted lower in recent
weeks, and while these are not the same as the RBNZ’s
official forecasts, they may be a useful guide as to the
direction of change.
Sentiment-based activity indicators: The NZIER’s
Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion was mixed. Firms
were more optimistic about the coming months, but
recent performance has been more subdued and
continues to fall short of expectations. Hiring and

investment intentions picked up compared to last
quarter, but more firms cited a lack of demand rather
than capacity as their main constraint on growth.
Meanwhile, the monthly PMI surveys have shown ongoing
strength in manufacturing, with more modestly positive
readings for service industries.
Housing market: Housing market conditions have
remained soft. House sales have fallen a further 4%
since July, the average time to sell remains elevated,
and prices have continued to edge lower. The RBNZ had

already assumed a modest decline in prices over 2026,
and the recent performance has been broadly in line with
their assumption.
Labour market: Filled jobs have picked up in the last
two months, having been flat through the first half of this
year. That’s in line with the RBNZ’s forecast of a rise in
employment and a tick down in the unemployment rate
to 5.5% in the September quarter. The QSBO showed
some tightening in the availability of skilled workers and a

lift in hiring intentions. Net migration has been in line with
the RBNZ’s assumptions.
Inflation: Annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in the
September quarter, below the RBNZ’s forecast of
3.9%. The downside surprise was on non-tradables
inflation, while tradables inflation was in line with the
RBNZ’s forecast. Underlying inflation pressures remain
elevated, with the various measures of core inflation
close to or above the top of the RBNZ’s 1-3% target

range. Since the September MPS forecasts were finalised,
oil prices have surged higher again, with petrol prices
setting new record highs. The Dubai crude spot price
is currently around $110/bbl, compared to the RBNZ’s
assumption of an average price of around $84/bbl for the
December quarter.
Inflation expectations: The QSBO showed that firms’
expectations of costs and prices remain elevated, but
lower than they were in the June quarter. ANZ’s monthly

survey showed a small dip in inflation expectations
among both businesses and consumers, although the
survey period may have pre-dated the worst of the
latest resurgence in fuel prices (the next updates will be
released just after the OCR review).
Trade: The June quarter terms of trade were in line with
the RBNZ’s forecast. Prices for New Zealand’s commodity
exports have generally remained firm in recent months,
with a pickup in dairy prices against some easing in beef

and lamb prices.
Financial conditions: Domestic financial conditions have
provided mixed signals since the September MPS. Long-
term interest rates have risen sharply along with global
markets, which the RBNZ may regard as an unwarranted
tightening of financial conditions to the extent that it
hasn’t reflected domestic economic conditions. Most
fixed-term mortgage rates have risen by 5-15 basis points
since the September MPS, but rates for the popular

two-year term have actually fallen a little as lenders
have looked to compete in this space. Meanwhile, the
New Zealand dollar has fallen sharply and the trade-
weighted index is just over 3% below the RBNZ’s assumed
average for the December quarter. If sustained, this will
add to imported inflation pressures over the year ahead.

We expect a 25bp hike on 28 October by the Reserve Bank of
New Zealand. Since the dovish September meeting, rising oil
prices, higher front-end rates globally and weakness in the NZ
dollar all point to risks of inﬂation overshooting 4.0% in 4Q. Oﬀ-
meeting commentary has supported market bets on another
hike, and we expect a hawkish-leaning statement
Francesco Pesole
Auckland, New Zealand

### Page 2

In September, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised rates to
2.75% but pushed back against expectations for a large tightening
cycle. Its projections pointed to the next hike only in December or
1Q27 and a 3.25% peak in 2028-2029.
The picture has turned increasingly hawkish since then. We now
expect a hike on 28 October, in line with market pricing,
accompanied by a hawkish statement. We expect another hike in
either December or February, with a slight preference for February,

and see chances of another move later in 2027. Our terminal rate
estimate is 3.25-3.5%.
Markets input has been hawkish for the
RBNZ
Source: ING, Macrobond
A lot has changed since September
Third-quarter inﬂation slowed from 4.1% to 3.7%, below both the
RBNZ’s 3.9% estimate and our 3.8% forecast. Non-tradable CPI, a
measure of core inﬂation, came in at 3.3% versus the RBNZ’s 3.6%
estimate. In isolation, those numbers would have reinforced
September’s dovish message and argued for an October hold.

Other developments, however, have argued for earlier action:
1. Higher energy prices. The RBNZ’s September projections
assumed oil prices would gradually decline. Instead, they have
risen around 20% since the September meeting. That has
materially increased the chances of 4Q inﬂation exceeding

4.0%, compared with the RBNZ’s 3.9% estimate, and remaining
stickier afterwards.
2. Weaker Kiwi dollar. The trade-weighted value of NZD tumbled
4% in September, and is at the lowest since 2009 (chart below).
The RBNZ has been one of the culprits behind a weak NZD, and
while it doesn’t normally react to FX moves, the depreciation is
increasing imported inﬂation risk.
3. Resilient growth. Second-quarter GDP surprised to the upside
at 2.6% YoY, against the RBNZ’s 2.1% estimate. Activity surveys

suggest growth may remain around 2.0% in both 3Q and 4Q,
leaving the RBNZ more room to ﬁght inﬂation with higher rates.
4. Hawkish repricing elsewhere. US two-year swap rates have
risen around 35bp since the start of September, with NZD 2yr
swaps following 33bp higher. Australia’s increase was smaller
at 15bp, but so is the starting point for rates. The RBA raised
rates again to 4.6% in September and kept the door open for
more.

acknowledged market pricing and highlighted upside risks to the

The focus on 4Q inﬂation seemed to us to be a signal for markets
to look past the softer 3Q data. Governor Anna Breman reinforced
that message today, highlighting concerns about a higher 4Q
ﬁgure and eﬀectively neutralising any dovish impact from the
release. Markets now price in 21bp for this meeting. The proximity

of New Zealand’s 7 November general election does not seem a
suﬃcient reason for the RBNZ to deliver a big dovish surprise by
holding rates.
Hawkish hike to help NZD
We see little reason for the RBNZ to hike and pair the move with
dovish signals. Renewed focus on the currency means its
communication may be more market-aware than usual, while
resilient growth allows it to err on the hawkish side.
There are no new projections at this meeting, giving the RBNZ more

ﬂexibility to adjust its policy message through the statement
without sounding committal on the rate path. We think the
message will focus on a) keeping the door open to a December
hike; and b) signalling that the hiking cycle can be longer and faster
than previously estimated. We expect this to validate market
pricing for around 75bp of tightening by end-1Q27.
We saw good upside potential for NZD in our latest monthly FX
update, as the currency looked cheap by short-term valuation

metrics and there was room for further hawkish repricing in NZD
front-end rates. Our 0.570 one-month target has already been
reached, and we admit further NZD/USD gains depend more on
global/US developments than domestic factors. Still, the pair is
roughly 1% undervalued in the short term, and some emerging
USD idiosyncratic weakness around AI concerns, combined with a
hawkish RBNZ hike, can help build a stronger ﬂoor and leave
potential for further exploration.

Document 391: TPCA 2026 quick takeaways - 大尺寸、高層數、整合多顆內埋Bridge _ 矽電容、多層芯板

Source PDF: all report pdf/TPCA 2026 quick takeaways - 大尺寸、高層數、整合多顆內埋Bridge _ 矽電容、多層芯板.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

台灣股市｜科技 
事件分析

Fubon Research 
2026 年 10 月 20 日

資料來源: TPCA

黃韻庭 
yunting.huang@fubon.com 
886-2-27815995#37117

TPCA 2026 quick takeaways

大尺寸、高層數、整合多顆內埋 Bridge / 矽電容、多層芯板 
1.高階載板:大尺寸、高層數、整合內埋元件（Bridge / 矽電容） 、多層芯板 
2. EMIB-T 載板需極高對位精度 
3. CPO（Co-Packaged Optics）IC 載板扮演著極關鍵的高密度整合平台角色

高階載板: 大尺寸、高層數、整合多顆內埋元件（Bridge / 矽電容） 、多層芯板 
AI 資料中心持續提升運算效能，主要面臨高速互連、高效供電及熱管理三大技術挑
戰，隨著晶片整合規模擴大、功耗增加，PCB 與 IC 載板必須朝更大尺寸、更高密度
及更多層數發展，並同步提升訊號完整性、電源完整性及翹曲控制能力。 隨著 AI 
GPU、CPO（光電共封裝）與高階 Networking Switch（網路交換器）的晶片尺寸
與功耗爆發，載板尺寸正朝向「極大尺寸（Extreme Large Body Size） 」發展。極
大尺寸必須搭配加厚的 Multi-Layer Core（如 2 層、3 層至 6 層 Core） ，以提供
足夠的結構剛性。整合多顆內埋元件（Bridge / 矽電容）可進一步化電源與訊號完
整性。先進封裝及伺服器系統加速升級，也對 IC 載板提出更高技術要求。

載板尺寸正朝向「極大尺寸（Extreme Large Body Size） 」發展。 
在高階載板（IC Substrates）產業中，隨著 AI GPU、CPO（光電共封裝）與高階 
Networking Switch（網路交換器） 的晶片尺寸與功耗爆發 ，Body Size（載板尺寸）
正朝向「極大尺寸（Extreme Large Body Size） 」發展。過去 100 x 100 mm 即被
視為極大尺寸；目前業界已進入 110 x 110 mm 量產階段，並推進至 150 x 130 
mm 與 150 x 150 mm（如 CPO 載板） 的樣品開發與驗證階段。針對未來高效能 
Switch 與 CPO 產品，載板尺寸已進一步升級至 230 x 230 mm 的極限規格。當
尺寸擴大至 100 x 100 mm 以上時，載板總層數普遍達到 15~20 層以上；若推
進至 230 x 230 mm，整體結構更需要高達 30 多層（如 16 + 2 + 16 ） 。極大尺
寸必須搭配加厚的 Multi-Layer Core（如 2 層、3 層至 6 層 Core） ，以提供足夠
的結構剛性，並增設電源/接地層 （Power/Ground） 以維護電源完整性 （PI） 。在大
尺寸載板上，業界不再追求極端微細線路。因為面積足夠容納線路，過細過密的線
路反而容易引發訊號干擾；研發重心已轉向「大尺寸、高層數、整合多顆內埋元件
（Bridge / 矽電容） 」 。

圖表 1: IC substrate , EBS( extreme large body size)

資料來源: Unimicron 
李耿聿 
aaron.gy.li@fubon.com 
886-2-27815995#37107

### Page 2

台灣股市｜科技 
事件分析

Advanced substrate - Embedded Passive 埋入式被動元件載板 
1)因應高功耗需求：隨著 AI GPU 等高算力晶片的功耗與用電量急劇增加，晶片需
要更龐大且穩定的電力供應; 2)大幅縮短傳輸距離：傳統設計中，電源需由載板最底
層走較長的路徑傳遞至上層晶片；埋入矽電容後，電能可預先儲存在電容中並近距
離直接供電，傳輸路徑顯著縮短 以及 3)優化電源與訊號完整性：近距離供電能有
效提供更穩定的電源供應，優化  Power Integrity（PI，電源完整性） 與 Signal 
Integrity（SI，訊號完整性） ，促進電訊號傳遞。 IC substrate (Flip Chip BGA) 
Embedded Passive 可改善電源完整性（PI）與縮短供電路徑。矽電容主要埋設於
載板的增層 （Build-up layer） 結構中。位置偏向接近載板最上層，以便最直接地為
上方晶片提供電力支援 。Roadmap 顯示 2026 量產結構為 10/2/10; 8/6/8，NPI 為
18/2/18; 10/n/10，2027 為 14/n/14，2028 為 16/n/16，最大板身從 93x93mm
成長至 150x150mm。

圖表 2: IC substrate ,Embedded passive

資料來源: Unimicron

EMIB-T 載板需極高對位精度 
Bridge / Interposer（矽橋/中介層）埋入載板內部以提升晶片間傳輸效率。主要技
術門檻: 1) 極高對位精度 （Alignment）：放置 Bridge 等微小元件時，必須精確與
上下層的微細線路及盲孔 （Via） 對齊，對位稍有偏差即失效。2)異材質結合與可靠
度（Material Bonding & Reliability）：矽元件與載板有機材料間的接合面容易產生
氣泡 ， 在後續熱驗證中易引發分層 （Delamination） 問題 。3)不可重工 （No Rework）
與高成本 ：元件一旦埋入便無法取出一重工；若對位或接合不良導致瑕疵，整塊  
Panel/載板即宣告報廢，產生極高的成本風險 ; 4) 事前檢測（Known Good Die, 
KGD）： 為避免將不良元件埋入導致載板報廢 ， 元件在嵌入前必須先通過嚴格的良率
檢測 。5)多顆矽橋 （Multi-Bridge） 倍增的良率挑戰:隨著高階晶片尺寸 （Body Size），
載板內部需要同時內埋多顆 Bridge 元件。多顆內埋使對位與結合難度呈倍數級上
升。

### Page 3

台灣股市｜科技 
事件分析

Multi-Layer Core（多層芯板 / 多層 Core） 載板 
為高階載板（IC Substrate ）為因應高效能運算與 AI 晶片需求所採用的核心結構
技術。與傳統單一大塊單層 Core 的設計不同，Multi-Layer Core 是將兩層、三層
或更多層的 Core（C） ，中間透過 PP（Prepreg，半固化片） 黏合疊加在一起，使
載板中間的芯板結構顯著加厚。Multi-Layer Core（多層芯板 / 多層 Core） 載板 
可帶來, 1)電源完整性（PI）與訊號完整性（SI）：因應 AI GPU 與伺服器晶片功耗
及用電量大增，多層 Core 能設置更多 Power / Ground（電源層與接地層） 來分
流電流 ， 為單一晶片提供更穩定充足的電力 ， 優化 PI 與 SI。2) 結構強度與板彎控
制（Warping & Planarity）：加厚的 Core 結構能提升整體載板的剛性與平整度，
協助抵御熱應力與板彎形變 。雖然市場曾討論 「Core 變厚是否需轉用 Glass Core」，
但 Glass Core 現階段面臨易碎、價格高昂且缺乏成熟量產供應鏈的限制，因此現
階段高階載板仍以 Multi-Layer Core 的有機材料疊合方案為主。

圖表 3: IC substrate, Multi-layer core

資料來源: Unimicron

CPO（Co-Packaged Optics）IC 載板扮演著極關鍵的高密度整合平台角色 
由於 CPO 載板周邊需要配置光學模組與多種週邊零件，導致其 Body Size（載板
尺寸）比一般 GPU 載板更大，且趨勢是越做越大。目前 CPO 載板正積極推進至 
150 x 150 mm （目前多處於開發與送樣驗證階段） ；未來針對高階 Networking 
Switch，尺寸更可延伸至 230 x 230 mm 的極限規格。配合大尺寸設計，CPO 載
板的層數普遍落在 15~20 層以上 （甚至高達 20 幾層至 30 幾層） 。CPO 載板集
成了極大尺寸（Large Body Size） 、多層芯板（Multi-Layer Core） 、微孔（Micro 
Via）以及內埋元件（Embedded Bridge / 矽電容）等多項高階技術。雖然光學連
接器型態可能改變，但載板本身依然不可或缺，持續作為晶片與光電模組間供電與
訊號承載的核心基底。

### Page 4

台灣股市｜科技 
事件分析

圖表 4:IC substrate, CPO (Co-package optics)

資料來源: Unimicron

台灣股市｜科技

評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差
